### F9 — frozen prospective source-complete evidence study (runner declaration, 2026-09-26)

F9 tests whether exact source information remains coherently retained and exposed through the host records/report-group API as source length grows. The failed F8 single-document endpoint and its unattempted dependent arms remain unchanged. F8's twenty-page recording is only a **post hoc exploratory resource anchor under this new endpoint**. The new endpoint does not establish a complete clinical document, convenient browser review, patient confirmation, acceptance or production readiness.

The new evaluator requires operational completion, all literal source pages retained, six source facts, one source-bound coherent report group, supported printed-person/report anchors, resolvable same-proposal context references, correct original URLs, source-consistent page claims and no unsupported or duplicate critical assertions. Every normalized literal segment must occur on its claimed actual PDF page; a page counts complete only when its entire decoded text is retained. Numeric/boolean payload additions and unknown clinical keys are rejected rather than ignored. Clinical negation, numeric value and unit, correction wording, temporal role and dates are checked against source facts. A historical comparison can remain undated; safe unknown identity is allowed and reported separately. Raw submitted payloads and populated clinical fields are bound to returned host mappings, with the host's empty-string representation of null date allowed. Mandatory independent review also inspects mapped fields absent from raw clinical proposals, all narrative assertions, durable proposal bytes and runtime/queue state.

## Four-hour investigation synthesis — 2026-09-26

The declared F8 and F9 conditional protocols have ended and all actual live outcomes have independent terminal and accounting verification. Final whole-scope execution/completeness review is pending. The owner allocated one maximum four-hour elapsed window, not a requirement to spend four hours or permission to retry failed gates. Both live protocols ended before any time or token ceiling. No new live experiment is authorized by these outcomes, and no further endpoint will be relaxed to dispatch the eighty-page holdout.

**Useful result:** exact source retention removed the observed rereading loop in these two native-text fixtures. The twenty- and forty-page attempts read every page exactly once and retained all six authored clinical findings in coherent review groups. All previously read page text persisted in model requests. Independent offline tests also exercised acknowledged-evidence replay across an ordinary context boundary with eighty mocked pages; neither live attempt crossed a context boundary. This supports the mechanism as a candidate for further development, not a replicated causal claim or production qualification.

**Two different failed requirements:** F8 expected one complete clinical document and instead got a header plus six supported observations. The normal app permits such components. Its old score stays failed. F9 separately demanded complete literal text of every page in emitted proposal evidence; forty pages produced seven fully transcribed pages and omitted thirty-three administrative continuations. All omitted pages were independently checked to contain no additional clinical finding, all originals remain intact, and no unsupported critical claim was found. F9's four other automatic diagnostics were false positives caused by valid inherited context and host label projection; they are disclosed and do not represent four product defects. The genuine all-page failure remains after that diagnosis.

**What cannot be estimated:** eighty pages was not attempted in either conditional protocol and no qualified numerical prediction was created. Successful long-report token cost, latency, quality, live context-turnover behavior and a200-page projection remain NOT_ESTIMABLE under these declared gates. The observed forty/twenty ratios—2.8682× total tokens,1.9167× requests and1.4453× runtime—are descriptive, content/publication-confounded comparisons. They neither establish linear scaling nor justify silently completing unexecuted matrix cells. The existing120-cell projection register is unchanged.

**What remains open:** complete clinical-document assembly if needed by the product; the appropriate completeness contract for selective clinical extraction versus literal administrative-page transcription; evaluator support for valid inherited context and label normalization; independent live turnover/held-out scaling; precise machine-qualified provenance and selected-person review. These are recorded requirements/questions, not newly authorized work. Identity pending is safe abstention; all candidates remain unaccepted and host clinical extraction/review states remain conservative. No OCR, fingerprinting or other earlier result is revised, and no production/provider gate closes.

**Accounting and bounded execution:** this investigation made70 real requests and used3,769,336 known tokens, with612,480 cached input already included. Summed active inference duration is11.897438minutes; elapsed preparation, review and documentation also count against the unchanged04:44:23→08:44:23UTC window. Across the retained whole study there are1,309 attempts and43,530,025 known tokens, with the same seven historical usage-unknown attempts. No missing charge is zero-filled. Both sole controllers and the isolated proxy are stopped. Git remains read-only, prototypes isolated and all original/partial evidence preserved. The timers will be deleted only after final independent scope review and closure are recorded.

## Four-hour investigation final independent closure

### Final independent execution review — four-hour rereading investigation, 2026-09-26

**Disposition: POSITIVE for completing and extending the declared bounded investigation. Completeness: 9/10. Extendedness: 8/10.** These scores assess execution, independent verification, useful diagnosis and truthful reporting. They are not passing extraction-quality scores, successful scaling validation or approval of the prototype. The separate earlier bounded F1–F7 review remains unchanged.

I reviewed the owner's request to investigate the rereading problem and scaling within one maximum four-hour elapsed window, the subsequently fixed conditional F8/F9 protocols, all actual live receipts, the frozen declarations, raw accounting and independently rederived source/runtime evidence. I also read the final four-hour synthesis and the appended proposal, projection-register and public-worklist updates. The synthesis accurately distinguishes completed execution from negative scientific outcomes and unattempted dependent arms.

**What was completed.** The team isolated the original text-eviction mechanism, implemented an opt-in prototype in an isolated checkout, preserved ordinary source/progress/context/acceptance limits, and tested exact-text retention and acknowledged-source replay through real bridge and runtime seams. Offline tests exercised eighty fictional pages and two contexts, including source/acknowledgment isolation and oversize refusal. The original twenty-page live fixture then read all pages once, retained all six findings and stopped at its failed one-complete-document prerequisite. Independent contract reconciliation explained why the app permits supported components without changing that frozen result.

The separately declared F9 prospectively tested forty pages under an all-pages literal-evidence endpoint, after adversarial evaluator review and a new immutable declaration. It also read every page once and retained all six findings. Its proposal payloads contain only seven complete page texts; the remaining thirty-three administrative pages are preserved in the original and were read but were not fully transcribed into the proposal payloads. The declared prerequisite therefore genuinely fails. The frozen controller did not dispatch eighty pages. Every actual F8/F9 attempt has independent raw request/response, usage, original-source, durable-proposal, stopped-runtime and narrative verification. There is no actual live result still awaiting verification.

**Findings and limitations were handled honestly.** Early F8/F9 pre-use findings concerning acknowledgment, exposure attribution, deadlines, manual gates, missing cached usage and unsupported evaluator fields were corrected and retested before their respective freezes. Four additional F9 diagnostics discovered at its terminal are false positives: the app validly inherits report/subject anchors through same-proposal context and derives a document display label from its explicit title. Independent production-resolver and mapping checks establish that these are not four product defects. They are preserved and explained in the documentation; the genuine missing-full-page criterion independently remains failed. Correcting these helper limitations for future reuse is open engineering work, not necessary to reinterpret the current terminal or permission to advance the failed study.

This is why the scores are below ten. The positive evaluator alternatives did not cover every valid application representation before F9, and the requested successful long-document extrapolation could not be obtained. Neither live attempt crossed a context boundary. There are no repetitions, randomized causal comparison, qualified eighty-page holdout or successful 200-page forecast. The current findings support a retention mechanism candidate and identify an unresolved product completeness contract. They do not establish a universal unit-size winner, clinical-document assembly, automatic identity association, convenient browser review or production readiness.

**Ending now is justified.** Both predeclared conditional protocols reached their stated terminal conditions before any token or time cap. This is not abandoning an arm because an arbitrary operational guard fired: forty-page traversal actually finished, and the separately specified output requirement failed. The eighty-page prerequisite is unsatisfied, even after the evaluator false positives are independently diagnosed. Running it now as a successful-calibration validation would require changing the declaration or inventing a new endpoint. Merely having unused time does not justify repeated endpoint changes or attempts until a pass appears. A future study could choose a product-approved selective-extraction endpoint and qualify a compatible evaluator prospectively, but that is a new design decision, not an unresolved execution obligation inside F8/F9. No additional provider or OCR run is required to close this bounded investigation honestly.

**Accounting, preservation and safeguards.** The new investigation made seventy real provider attempts and used 3,769,336 known tokens, including 612,480 cached input tokens already counted within input. Its summed active-run duration is 11.897438 minutes; preparation, review and documentation also count against the shared elapsed window. Full retained study totals are 1,309 attempts and 43,530,025 known tokens, including 10,700,928 known cached input tokens. Seven historical usage-unknown attempts remain unknown. The twenty-page post hoc anchor is counted once, mocks are excluded and no monetary charge or missing usage is invented.

At final review I rechecked all eighteen source/twelve artifact F8 bindings and thirty source/thirteen artifact F9 bindings. Both original declarations remain unchanged, as does the deadline hash. The original result/endpoint files and earlier independent review entries remain intact; the reviewed reports are present verbatim in the appended public ledger. Git remains read-only, changes remain uncommitted, prototypes are isolated and no production default was selected. The original 120-cell register, its 106 unattempted cells, prior OCR/fingerprinting findings, standalone T/CRS requirements and provider/production acceptance gates are not closed by this review.

The single shared clock is still 04:44:23 start, 07:44:23 last dispatch and 08:44:23 hard finish UTC; it was never reset across agents, preparation or F9. The offline mechanism checkpoint was met. Both sole controllers exited, their locks were released and the isolated proxy plus original app containers are stopped. No hidden eighty-page attempt or pre-dispatch prediction exists. Independent advance receipts are explicitly negative. The deadline monitor and hard-stop timer are still active with the unchanged bounds at this review, as requested; the coordinator should delete them only after recording this closure so they cannot restart completed work.

**No material execution or reporting finding remains open for this conditional scope.** The remaining material scientific/product questions are explicitly listed in the synthesis and maintained worklist: appropriate completeness semantics, full-document assembly if needed, compatible evaluator inheritance/normalization, live turnover, held-out resource scaling and identity/provenance review. This is a product decision after the recorded runs, not a new result or a retrospective change to their criteria. All earlier recordings, verification entries and failed grades remain unchanged.

F9 read all forty pages and retained all six clinical findings, but only seven full page texts were present in its emitted proposal evidence. The thirty-three omitted administrative page transcriptions matter under the clarified requirement even though the original and temporary model read history remained intact. The run did not establish complete durable searchable text; clinical-fact completeness alone is insufficient. The earlier suggestion that a selective-extraction endpoint could suffice is superseded for total text retention.

The product requirement does not demand copying all text into each clinical proposal or retaining one monolithic clinical document. A separate durable, source-bound complete-text representation with authorized retrieval can meet it. Future verification must account for every readable region, preserve explicit unreadable exceptions and demonstrate retrieval after reload/recovery without another AI extraction. This clarification does not restart the closed study or turn the unattempted eighty-page arm into a result.
